Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the poincare object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 713. The moduli space has dimension at most 831 with 3 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_713(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 713. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^831(S, F) is trivial. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
